# ex_25 -- descriptor ablation of HDF (graph size + diameter; reviewer comment R3.3)
# Paper: Supplementary Section S6.3, Supplementary Table S9
# Source: graph_hdc revision/consolidated, _slurm_ex_25.py regenerated in a copy (corrected HOPV15 indices). Equal to
#         the used KCIST files _ex25/hdf_0.txt, hdf_1.txt, fp.txt (jobs 51162-51164, 2026-10-12) except the 40 HOPV15
#         lines, whose TARGET_INDEX is now PCE 3 / Voc 0 (old 5 / 4 = band gaps); those rows and ClogP (label scaling
#         fixed in predict_molecules.py) were re-run with these exact lines in KCIST job 51196 (_slurm_fix_targets.py).
#         Prefix ex_25_desc.
# Runs: primer_data 7, primer 103, main 390
#       = 500: hdf_noglobal and morgan_desc on all 17 Table 1 targets x 10 seeds (340), hdf and morgan on the 8 targets
#       that ex_14 leaves out x 10 seeds (160)
# Protocol: MLP only (neural_net2 (100,100), lr 1e-3), seeds 0-9 (split, network and HDF codebook per seed: CODEBOOK_SEED
#   not set), splits of ex_14. Arms: hdf (standard encoder, 2048, depth 2), hdf_noglobal (GRAPH_ATTRIBUTES False),
#   morgan (radius 2, 2048 bits), morgan_desc (+ heavy-atom count and diameter, each / dataset max; GRAPH_DESCRIPTORS).
# Analysis: python analyze_ex_25.py   (-> _ex25/results_ex_25_desc.csv, contrasts_ex_25_desc.csv/.md,
#           descriptor_ablation_mlp_ex_25_desc.tex = Table S9; 4 paired contrasts per target, Holm-corrected)
# Cost: KCIST, 4 small nodes, ~3 h (2 HDF shards + 2 Morgan shards); dominated by the QM9 and COMPAS-3X runs.
# Notes: REQUIRES the hdf and morgan arms on the 9 ex_14 targets (HOPV15 PCE, FreeSolv, BACE, Lipophilicity, AqSolDB,
#   COMPAS-3X gap, QM9 ZPVE/U0/gap): analyze_ex_25.py takes them from the ex_14 archives (prefix ex_14_gnn, HDF arm)
#   and ex_19 (prefix ex_19_fp, Morgan arm) and uses their splits as reference -> run the ex_14 and ex_19 protocols
#   first (not duplicated here). Stage "primer_data" = the first HDF line of every dataset and "primer" = the first
#   line of every other (dataset, seed, descriptors) encoding cache key (as the generator's primer block); on KCIST
#   the dataset caches already existed. Datasets via chem_mat_data (qm9_smiles, compas_3x, hopv15_exp, aqsoldb,
#   freesolv, bace_reg, lipophilicity).
# @stage primer_data
python predict_molecules__hdc.py --__DEBUG__="False" --__PREFIX__="'ex_25_desc'" --SEED="0" --NUM_TEST="0.1" --NUM_TRAIN="1.0" --NUM_DATA="1.0" --DATASET_NAME="'qm9_smiles'" --DATASET_TYPE="'regression'" --NOTE="'qm9_dipole'" --NN_NUM_WORKERS="0" --TARGET_INDEX="3" --EMBEDDING_SIZE="2048" --NUM_LAYERS="2" --ENCODING_MODE="'continuous'" --DEVICE="'cpu'" --SPECTRUM="'unit'" --MODELS="['neural_net2']" --NN_HIDDEN_LAYER_SIZES="(100, 100)" --NN_LEARNING_RATE_INIT="0.001" --BIDIRECTIONAL="True" --HYDROGEN_COUNT="'total'"
